Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the parabolic structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 241. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 233 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on parabolic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 241. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 233. QED.

Theorem 217.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on parabolic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 135. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 207 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 135 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the parabolic structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 207. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
